To further preserve semantic structures critical for downstream interpretation, GACR integrates Geo-Contextual Prior Alignment (GCPA) to constrain the reconstruction within a semantic manifold induced by a Vision Foundation Model (VFM). Consequently, GACR strictly maintains the spatial-semantic integrity of complex landscapes. Extensive experiments across six CR datasets and twelve downstream tasks demonstrate that GACR produces superior reconstruction quality while consistently improving downstream task accuracy. The code is available at https://github.com/wzy6055/GACR.

</details>


### [190] [EAGLE-360: Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration in 360$^\circ$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02479)
*Jingtao Xu,Zizhuo Lin,Jianwen Sun,Yi Yang,Yawei Luo*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EAGLE-360 提出了一种面向360°全景环境的主动视觉搜索框架，通过全局到局部的探索范式、RoPE Rolling位置编码以及大规模数据集与SFT+GRPO训练，在目标检测精度上实现近8倍提升，并显著提高了探索效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在全景视觉搜索中存在以下问题：1) 难以建模全景特有的极地畸变和连续圆柱拓扑结构，导致目标检测精度下降；2) 现有方法依赖碎片化的局部视角，缺乏全局全景先验，导致近视、低效的探索和错误恢复能力差。

Method: 1) 提出EAGLE-360全局到局部探索框架，利用全局先验建立初始整体视角，逐步迭代推理缩小搜索空间；2) 引入RoPE Rolling坐标偏移位置编码，建模全景连续拓扑；3) 构建包含14000+张4K全景图和70000+轮VQA对话的大规模数据集；4) 采用SFT结合GRPO的训练流程，激发复杂空间推理和工具调用能力。

Result: 在360°视觉搜索任务上达到新SOTA，相比基线模型精度提升近8倍，探索效率显著提升。

Conclusion: EAGLE-360通过全局到局部探索范式与专门的全景建模设计，有效解决了现有MLLM在全景主动视觉搜索中的根本性限制，验证了该范式的有效性。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated exceptional capabilities in standard visual understanding, adapting them for active visual search in 360$^\circ$ panoramic environments exposes fundamental limitations. Specifically, standard MLLMs struggle to effectively model inherent panoramic properties, such as severe polar distortion and continuous cylindrical topologies, which significantly degrades target detection accuracy. Consequently, existing panoramic search methods attempt to compensate by relying heavily on fragmented local viewpoints. Burdened by rigid initialization and a lack of global panoramic priors, these approaches suffer from myopic, inefficient exploration and struggle with robust error recovery when targets are out of view. To overcome these challenges, we propose EAGLE-360, a novel Embodied Active Global-to-Local Exploration framework. Rather than performing exhaustive local searches, EAGLE-360 leverages global priors to establish an initial holistic perspective, iteratively reasoning and progressively narrowing the search space. Architecturally, we adapt RoPE Rolling, a coordinate-shifting positional encoding mechanism, to seamlessly model the continuous topologies of panoramas. To facilitate this paradigm, we construct the large-scale EAGLE-360 dataset, comprising 14,000+ 4K panoramas and 70,000+ rounds of high-quality VQA dialogues. By employing a training pipeline that integrates Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT) with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), we effectively elicit complex spatial reasoning and tool-calling capabilities. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EAGLE-360 establishes a new state-of-the-art for 360$^\circ$ visual search, achieving nearly an 8-fold increase in accuracy over the base model while significantly enhancing exploration efficiency.

</details>


### [191] [Combating Textual Noise and Redundancy: Entropy-Aware Dense Visual Token Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02484)
*Xuehui Wang,Xuankun Yang,Wei Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EADP通过熵感知去噪和子模最大化结构压缩，解决了密集指令下视觉token剪枝中文本噪声干扰和特征碎片化问题，实现了更优的精度-效率平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法在密集指令和细粒度查询场景下无法保留关键线索，原因是：（1）文本噪声广泛分散，污染了密集跨模态评分；（2）标准token选择导致特征碎片化。

Method: 提出EADP框架：（1）利用统计熵量化并过滤文本噪声，得到鲁棒的细粒度指令相关性分数；（2）将token选择转化为带空间先验的子模最大化问题，确保整体且非冗余的视觉表示。

Result: 在严格的token预算下鲁棒地保留细粒度视觉线索，在挑战性多模态基准上达到SOTA性能。

Conclusion: EADP通过将剪枝重构为结构化压缩问题，有效改善了VLM的精度-效率权衡。

Abstract: Visual token pruning is a crucial strategy for accelerating VLMs by compressing redundant image patches, yet existing methods often fail to preserve critical cues under dense instructions and fine-grained queries. In this paper, we investigate this failure and identify two underlying bottlenecks: the widespread dispersion of textual noise that corrupts dense cross-modal scoring, and the feature fragmentation inherent to standard token selection. To address these issues, we propose Entropy-Aware Dense Pruning (EADP), a framework that reformulates pruning as a structured compression problem. EADP first leverages statistical entropy to quantify and filter out textual noise, yielding a robust, fine-grained instruction relevance score. Subsequently, instead of naive Top-K selection, EADP casts token selection as a submodular maximization problem with a spatial prior, explicitly ensuring a holistic and non-redundant visual representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate that EADP improves the accuracy-efficiency trade-off of VLMs, robustly preserving fine-grained visual cues under strict token budgets while achieving SoTA performance on challenging multimodal benchmarks.

</details>


### [192] [GeoMix: Descriptor-Free Visual Localization via Global Context and Multi-Detector Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02486)
*Yejun Zhang,Xinjue Wang,Zihan Wang,Esa Rahtu,Juho Kannala*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GeoMix是一种无需描述符的2D-3D视觉定位框架，通过局部方向/距离嵌入、全局上下文节点和多检测器混合训练三个层次增强几何判别性，在多个基准上将描述符-free方法的旋转误差降低89%、平移误差降低90%，大幅缩小了与描述符-based方法的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无需描述符的视觉定位虽然避免了高维描述符存储、保护场景隐私并简化地图维护，但其精度远落后于基于描述符的管线。作者将这一差距归因于纯几何匹配中几何判别性不足：缺乏视觉外观时，现有方法未充分利用局部几何线索、缺乏关键点间的全局上下文，且过拟合于单一关键点检测器。

Method: GeoMix从三个层次增强几何判别性：(1) 局部层面：引入方向感知和距离感知嵌入，丰富邻域聚合的细粒度空间结构；(2) 全局层面：利用可学习的上下文节点通过交叉注意力聚合和分发场景级信息，解决局部感受野之外的歧义；(3) 训练层面：提出Mix-Training，利用检测器无关的几何空间在多个关键点检测器上联合学习表示，自然地实现多检测器训练。

Result: 在MegaDepth、Cambridge Landmarks、7Scenes和Aachen Day-Night数据集上，GeoMix在描述符-free方法中达到SOTA，相比之前最优方法，75百分位旋转误差降低89%，平移误差降低高达90%，并零样本泛化到未见过的检测器，显著缩小了与描述符-based管线的差距。

Conclusion: 通过多层次几何判别性增强和多检测器混合训练，GeoMix有效弥合了描述符-free与描述符-based视觉定位之间的性能鸿沟，验证了无需描述符的方法在精度和泛化性上的潜力。

Abstract: Descriptor-free visual localization eliminates high-dimensional descriptor storage, preserves scene privacy, and simplifies map maintenance, yet its accuracy still lags far behind descriptor-based pipelines. We identify this gap to insufficient geometric discriminability in geometry-only matching. Without visual appearance, current methods underutilize local geometry cues, lack the global context among keypoints, and overfit to a single keypoint detector. We further observe that descriptor-free matching naturally enables multi-detector training, as heterogeneous keypoints can be optimized in a shared geometry-only space without aligning descriptor spaces. Building on these insights, we propose GeoMix, a descriptor-free 2D-3D matching framework that strengthens geometric discriminability at three levels. Locally, directional and distance-aware embeddings enrich neighborhood aggregation with fine-grained spatial structure. Globally, learnable context nodes aggregate and redistribute scene-wide information via cross-attention to resolve ambiguities beyond local receptive fields. At the training level, Mix-Training exploits this detector-agnostic geometry space to learn representations across multiple keypoint detectors. Extensive experiments on MegaDepth, Cambridge Landmarks, 7Scenes, and Aachen Day-Night show that GeoMix sets a new state of the art among descriptor-free methods, reducing 75th-percentile rotation error by 89\% and translation error by up to 90\% over the previous best, while generalizing zero-shot to unseen detectors and narrowing the gap to descriptor-based pipelines. Code is available at $\href{https://github.com/YejunZhang/Geomix}{\text{this links}}$.

</details>


### [193] [Towards Robustness against Typographic Attack with Training-free Concept Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02494)
*Bohan Liu,Wenqian Ye,Guangzhi Xiong,Zhenghao He,Sanchit Sinha,Aidong Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练的机制可解释方法，通过采样解释和电路挖掘定位CLIP/ViT中编码词汇信息的注意力头，并通过简单干预（如调整注意力权重）有效提升对印刷攻击的鲁棒性，在多个LVLM上验证了泛化性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLIP模型作为LVLMs的基础视觉编码器，存在一个关键但未被充分探索的缺陷：图像中的无关文本会干扰视觉表示，使其偏向词汇含义而非真实视觉语义，这在安全关键应用（如自动驾驶）中构成重大风险。

Method: 提出一种无需训练的可解释性方法：(1) 基于采样的隐藏状态表示解释；(2) 定量归因各注意力头的语义vs词汇关注度；(3) 通过概率分析和电路挖掘，识别Vision Transformer中过度编码词汇信息的具体组件；(4) 对识别出的电路施加无需训练的简单干预（如选择性调整注意力权重）。

Result: 在目标分类任务上，该方法有效提升了对印刷攻击的鲁棒性，且优于有监督和无训练的防御方法。将该干预应用于多个SOTA LVLM的视觉编码器后，显著提升了RIO-Bench上印刷攻击干扰下的VQA准确率。

Conclusion: 通过机制可解释性方法定位并干预词汇编码电路，无需额外训练即可有效防御印刷攻击，方法具有通用性和可迁移性。

Abstract: Models trained via Contrastive Language-Image Pretraining (CLIP) serve as the foundational vision encoders for most modern Large Vision Language Models (LVLMs). Despite their widespread adoption, CLIP models exhibit a critical yet underexplored failure mode: irrelevant text appearing within images confounds visual representations, biasing them toward lexical meaning rather than true visual semantics. This robustness issue, commonly described as a Typographic Attack (TA), exposes a vulnerability that poses a significant risk to safety-critical applications such as autonomous driving. To achieve interpretable and effective robustness against TA, we propose a novel, training-free mechanistic interpretability method. Our method provides sampling-based interpretations of hidden state representations and quantitatively attributes semantic versus lexical focus to individual attention heads. Through probabilistic analysis and circuit mining, we isolate specific Vision Transformer (ViT) components that disproportionately encode lexical information, thereby identifying the mechanistic source of TA. We further show that simple interventions applied directly to the identified circuits, without any additional training, can substantially improve robustness against Typographic Attacks in object classification. These interventions, such as selective adjustment of attention weights, also outperform both supervised and training-free defense methods. Our experiments demonstrate that applying the proposed intervention to the vision encoders of several state-of-the-art LVLMs yields substantial gains in Visual Question Answering accuracy under Typographic Attack interference on RIO-Bench. These results confirm both the efficacy and the generalizability of our mechanistic approach. Code is released at https://github.com/Liu-524/SamplingTAR.

</details>


### [194] [Seek to Segment: Active Perception for Panoramic Referring Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02497)
*Song Tang,Shuming Hu,Xincheng Shuai,Henghui Ding,Yu-Gang Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了主动全景指代分割（APRS）任务，并设计了PanoSeeker智能体，通过结合视觉语言模型与EgoSphere空间视觉记忆，实现360°环境中的高效目标搜索与分割，在基准测试中显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的指代分割模型只能处理静态图像，无法应用于具身智能中智能体需要在连续360°环境中主动感知的场景。

Method: 提出PanoSeeker智能体，包含EgoSphere显式空间视觉记忆模块，将局部观测逐步整合为统一360°表示以规划高效无冗余搜索轨迹；通过专家标注的搜索轨迹数据集进行监督微调，再结合强化学习后训练优化探索效率。

Result: 在构建的APRS基准测试上，PanoSeeker在搜索效率和分割精度上显著优于改造后的SOTA基线方法。

Conclusion: 记忆增强的主动探索策略能有效解决360°环境中的指代分割问题，为具身智能中的主动视觉感知提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Existing referring segmentation models passively process static images captured from fixed perspectives, limiting their applicability in Embodied AI, where agents must perform active perception in the continuous 360$^\circ$ environments. To bridge this gap, we introduce a novel task: Active Panoramic Referring Segmentation (APRS). In this setting, an agent is required to adjust its viewing direction ($Δθ, Δφ$) to explore the 360$^\circ$ environment, seeking the object specified by a user instruction for segmentation. To tackle this challenging task, we propose PanoSeeker, a memory-augmented agent for efficient APRS. Rather than relying on heuristic scanning, PanoSeeker integrates a Vision-Language Model (VLM) with EgoSphere, an explicit spatial visual memory. By progressively integrating sequential local observations into a unified 360$^\circ$ representation, EgoSphere enables the agent to plan efficient and non-redundant search trajectories. Once the target is found, the agent performs active viewpoint alignment and outputs the segmentation mask. Furthermore, we curate an expert-annotated search trajectory dataset with memory timelines for Supervised Fine-Tuning, followed by Reinforcement Learning post-training to explicitly optimize PanoSeeker's exploration efficiency. Extensive experiments on our newly established APRS benchmark demonstrate that PanoSeeker achieves superior search efficiency and segmentation accuracy, significantly outperforming adapted state-of-the-art baselines.

</details>


### [195] [From SRA to Self-Flow: Data Augmentation or Self-Supervision?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02508)
*Dengyang Jiang,Mengmeng Wang,Harry Yang,Jingdong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文质疑Self-Flow中双时间步调度带来的性能提升源于token间跨噪声层级交互的观点，通过引入Attention Separation方法（阻断不同噪声层级token间的注意力），发现移除交互不仅无损性能甚至有所提升，证明改进主要来自数据增强效果。进一步表明Attention Separation本身通过将单张图像分割为多个有效训练部分来扩充训练数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究扩散Transformer训练中，从SRA到Self-Flow的改进（双时间步调度）背后的真正机制——究竟是token间跨噪声层级的交互，还是沿噪声维度的数据增强。

Method: 提出Attention Separation方法，在保留Self-Flow双时间步输入的同时，阻断不同噪声层级token之间的注意力交互，以此解耦跨层级交互与数据增强两种因素。

Result: 阻断跨噪声层级交互后性能未下降甚至提升，表明Self-Flow的改进主要来自数据增强；Attention Separation本身通过将单张图像分割为多个有效训练部分来扩充训练数据。

Conclusion: 将自表征对齐与双时间步和注意力分离增强相结合，在ImageNet上验证了该设计的有效性。

Abstract: Representation alignment has become an effective way to accelerate diffusion transformer training and improve generation quality. Recent self-alignment methods, such as SRA and Self-Flow, further remove the dependency on external pretrained encoders by constructing alignment within the diffusion model itself. However, the mechanism behind the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow, dual-time scheduling, remains under-examined: Self-Flow attributes its gain to interactions between tokens at different noise levels, where cleaner tokens help infer noisier ones. In this work, we revisit this explanation and ask whether the gain instead comes from data augmentation along the noise dimension. To disentangle these factors, we introduce Attention Separation, which preserves the same dual-timestep input as Self-Flow while blocking attention between tokens assigned to different noise levels. Surprisingly, removing such interaction does not degrade performance and can even improve it, suggesting that the improvement from SRA to Self-Flow mainly comes from data augmentation. Furthermore,We show that Attention Separation itself provides an augmentation effect by splitting a single image into multiple effective training parts to expand the training data. Based on these observations, we combine self-representation alignment with dual-timestep and attention-separation augmentation, and demonstrate the effectiveness of this design on ImageNet.

</details>


### [196] [PointDiT: Pixel-Space Diffusion for Monocular Geometry Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02515)
*Haofei Xu,Rundi Wu,Philipp Henzler,Nikolai Kalischek,Michael Oechsle,Fabian Manhardt,Marc Pollefeys,Andreas Geiger,Federico Tombari,Michael Niemeyer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极简的像素空间扩散Transformer架构，直接对原始3D点图块进行操作，无需复杂混合架构或潜在空间压缩，在单图像3D重建任务上超越现有潜在扩散模型，且对透明物体等模糊区域更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的单图像3D重建方法依赖复杂的混合架构、繁琐的损失函数，或将几何压缩到潜在空间以利用预训练潜在扩散模型，这些架构开销和复杂的损失设计是不必要的。

Method: 采用基于普通ViT的像素空间扩散Transformer，直接操作原始3D点图块，以预训练DINOv3的图像令牌为条件，扩散主干从头训练，无需点图分词器。

Result: 超越复杂的潜在扩散模型，且比混合替代方案更简单；生成更锐利的几何结构，在透明物体等高模糊区域更鲁棒。

Conclusion: 单图像3D重建中的复杂架构和损失设计是不必要的，极简的像素空间扩散方法即可取得更优性能。

Abstract: State-of-the-art single-image 3D reconstruction methods often rely on complex hybrid architectures and loss functions, or compress geometry into latent spaces in order to leverage pre-trained latent diffusion models. In this work, we show that such architectural overhead and intricate loss formulations are unnecessary. We introduce a minimalist pixel-space Diffusion Transformer, built on a plain ViT, that operates directly on raw 3D point map patches and is conditioned on image tokens from a pre-trained DINOv3. Unlike existing latent diffusion approaches, we train our diffusion backbone entirely from scratch, eliminating the need for point map tokenizers. Despite its simplicity, our approach surpasses complex latent-based diffusion models while remaining significantly simpler than hybrid alternatives. Notably, it produces sharper geometric structure and is more robust in highly ambiguous regions, such as transparent objects.

</details>


### [197] [Alignment Is All You Need For X-to-4D Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02516)
*Qiaowei Miao,Kehan Li,Yawei Luo,Yi Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Align4D提出了一个灵活的任意模态到4D生成的框架，通过视频引导4D运动、3D数据塑造4D几何，并引入目标距离对齐、运动-几何联合对齐和异步优化三大关键技术，在X4D和Consistent4D基准上达到了SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成扩散模型在图像、视频、3D内容生成上表现优异，但任意用户定义的模态到4D（X-to-4D）生成仍面临数据构建成本高和现有方法可扩展性有限两大挑战。

Method: 提出三个关键技术：(1) 目标距离对齐：搜索视频对齐目标距离(VAOD)和多视图对齐目标距离(MAOD)以协调4D渲染与视频及多视图扩散先验；(2) 运动-几何联合对齐：通过同步视频和3D输入约束已知和未知视角，确保4D生成一致性；(3) 异步优化：解耦高斯属性与变形网络训练以增强运动和几何保真度。同时提出X4D基准数据集。

Result: 在X4D和Consistent4D数据集上的实验表明，Align4D在X-to-4D生成任务上达到了最先进的质量和一致性。

Conclusion: Align4D成功地将任意模态输入转化为一致的视频-3D对，为多模态4D生成提供了一种灵活且高效的框架。

Abstract: Generative diffusion models excel at synthesizing high-quality images, videos, and 3D content under multimodal control. However, arbitrary user-defined modality-to-4D (X-to-4D) generation remains challenging due to the high cost of constructing diverse datasets and the limited scalability of existing methods. This paper presents Align4D, a flexible framework that translates any-modal input into coherent video-3D pairs, using video to guide 4D motion and 3D data to shape 4D geometry. Align4D introduces three key techniques: (1) Object Distance Alignment, which searches Video-Aligned and Multiview-Aligned Object Distances (VAOD/MAOD), respectively, to reconcile 4D renderings with video and the priors of multiview diffusion models; (2) Motion-Geometry Joint Alignment, which constrains known and unknown views through synchronized video and 3D inputs, ensuring consistent 4D generation; and (3) Asynchronous Optimization, which decouples Gaussian attribute and deformation network training to enhance motion and geometry fidelity. We further propose the X4D dataset, which integrates prompt, image, video, and 3D data for benchmarking. Experiments on X4D and Consistent4D demonstrate that Align4D achieves state-of-the-art quality and consistency in X-to-4D generation. Project page: https://miaoqiaowei.github.io/Align4D/.

</details>


### [198] [WorldDirector: Building Controllable World Simulators with Persistent Dynamic Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02517)
*Hanlin Wang,Hao Ouyang,Qiuyu Wang,Wen Wang,Qingyan Bai,Ka Leong Cheng,Yue Yu,Yixuan Li,Yihao Meng,Zichen Liu,Yanhong Zeng,Yujun Shen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: WorldDirector提出了一种解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成的可控视频世界模型框架，通过LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，实现持久动态物体记忆和任意视角探索，即使在物体长时间离开视野后也能保持其视觉身份一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型将物理动态与像素渲染纠缠在一起，依赖连续视觉观察来维持运动，导致物体离开视野后无法保持其视觉身份和外观稳定性。

Method: 显式解耦语义运动编排与视觉生成：利用LLM协调3D轨迹与相机运动，将编排后的轨迹作为控制信号输入视频生成模型。

Result: 支持合成复杂和扩展事件，具有前所未有的可控性和持久动态物体记忆，物体长时间离开视野后重新进入场景时仍能精确保持视觉身份。

Conclusion: 通过解耦设计，WorldDirector在可控性和持久动态物体记忆方面显著优于现有世界模型。

Abstract: We present WorldDirector, a highly controllable video world model framework designed for persistent dynamic object memory and unrestricted viewpoint exploration. Unlike existing world models that entangle physical dynamics with pixel rendering and rely on continuous visual observation to sustain motion, our framework explicitly decouples semantic motion orchestration from visual generation. By leveraging an LLM to coordinate 3D trajectories with camera movements and subsequently employing these orchestrated trajectories as control signals for video generation, our approach ensures strict physical logic and appearance stability, successfully preserving the exact visual identities of dynamic entities even when they re-enter the scene after prolonged periods out of view. Experimental results demonstrate that our method supports the synthesis of complex and extended events with unprecedented controllability and persistent dynamic object memory. Project Page: https://worlddirector.github.io/

</details>


### [199] [ProCal: Inference-Time Proposal Calibration for Open-Vocabulary Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01759)
*Jae-Ryung Hong,Ho-Joong Kim,Seong-Whan Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProCal（Proposal Calibration）方法，通过结合定位感知前景分数和背景感知抑制分数来校准分类分数，从而改进开放词汇目标检测中未见类别的定位质量，在OV-LVIS上提升APr +2.5。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开放词汇目标检测方法使用冻结VLM作为检测器骨干，但VLM的分类分数缺乏对物体位置和尺度的识别能力，导致对未见类别的定位和分类不够准确。

Method: 提出ProCal方法，在推理时计算提议先验（proposal prior），由两个分数组合而成：1）定位感知前景分数，判断提议是否包含物体区域；2）背景感知抑制分数，衡量提议与背景的相似程度。通过这种校准，抑制背景提议上的假阳性新类激活，并正确排序真正的新类提议。

Result: 应用于CLIPSelf ViT-L/14，在OV-LVIS上APr提升+2.5。分析表明，提议级定位感知重排序有效缓解了新类物体的排序校准偏差。

Conclusion: ProCal通过简单的推理时校准策略，有效提升了开放词汇目标检测中未见类别的定位质量，验证了利用VLM区分前景/背景能力来辅助检测的可行性。

Abstract: Open-vocabulary object detection aims to localize and classify objects beyond the fixed set of categories seen dur ing training. Recent open-vocabulary object detection methods improve localization and classification for unseen categories by leveraging a frozen VLM as a detector backbone. However, VLM classification score lacks recognizing position and scale of the object in an image. We observe that pretrained VLMs en able to classify foreground and background regions. According to this observation, we propose a simple inference-time Pro posal Calibration (ProCal) that improves localization quality of the classification score. ProCal computes a proposal prior by combining two scores: localization-aware foreground score and background-aware suppression score. Localization-aware foreground score captures whether a proposal contains an object area. Background-aware suppression score measures the extent to which the proposal resembles background. We analyze that ProCal suppresses false novel activation on background proposals and consistently ranks true novel proposals above background and partial novel proposals. Applied to CLIPSelf ViT-L/14, ProCal improves APr +2.5 on OV-LVIS. The analyses show that proposal-level localization-aware reranking effects to mitigate ranking miscalibration for novel objects.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [200] [Embedding Inference Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01276)
*Cedric Fitiavana Raelijohn,Sébastien Gambs,Jean-Francois Rajotte*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出嵌入推理攻击（EIA），在仅能观察到无序检索文档集的黑盒设置下，通过精心设计的查询识别IR系统使用的嵌入模型，并验证了该方法在包含重排序器或RAG系统下的有效性，同时探讨了相似度阈值等缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有嵌入模型反演攻击通常假设攻击者已知嵌入模型，但在实际黑盒场景中攻击者只能观察到检索结果而无排序或相似度分数，需要一种能在这种受限条件下识别嵌入模型的方法。

Method: 设计特定查询（tailored queries）作为判别性输入，通过观察系统返回的无序文档集合，从候选模型集中推断使用的嵌入模型；同时在包含重排序器和真实RAG系统中验证攻击有效性，并设计绕过LLM拒绝机制的查询。

Result: 在仅观察无序检索文档的黑盒条件下成功识别嵌入模型；该方法在包含重排序器的系统中仍保持判别力；在真实RAG系统中能绕过LLM的输入拒绝机制。

Conclusion: 黑盒IR系统对嵌入模型推断攻击存在脆弱性，即使仅有检索文档集无排序信息也能被攻击；重排序器无法完全防御；相似度阈值等缓解策略可提供一定保护。

Abstract: Embedding models are essential components of modern Information Retrieval (IR) systems, yet they are typically hidden behind APIs. Recent works have shown that dense IR system can lead to security vulnerabilities such as embedding inversion attacks. However, such attacks usually require that the attacker knows the embedding model for the attack to be applicable. In this paper, we study IR systems under a black-box setting in which the adversary observes only the unordered set of retrieved documents, without ranking or similarity scores. We demonstrate that in such contexts, tailored queries allow an adversary to identify which embedding model is in use from a set of known model candidate, which we coin as an embedding inference attack (EIA). We also show that certain queries remain discriminative even when the system includes a reranker as a potential defense mechanism. We further validate our method on a real Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) system, in which the tailored queries bypass the LLM's tendency to reject inputs it does not recognize as well-formed questions. Finally, we propose and evaluate other mitigation strategies such as similarity thresholds.

</details>


### [201] [Cognitive Firewall: A Proactive, Zero-Trust, Multi-Gate Framework for LLM Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01277)
*Michele Guida,Ruslan Shikhhamzayev,Sindhuja Penchala,Stefano Iannucci,Jiacheng Li,Shahram Rahimi,Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出认知防火墙(Cognitive Firewall)，一种将LLM安全评估分解为意图门、零信任上下文门、一致性门和输出风险门四个分类门控的主动运行时监督框架，通过升级机制而非分数平均进行门控决策组合，在多个越狱基准测试上将攻击成功率降至2%-14%，同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有运行时安全机制将提示或响应作为孤立消息评估，无法恢复跨轮次的累积意图、验证声称的权限或检测跨对话分解的有害目标，导致多轮策略可绕过单条消息的安全检测。

Method: 提出认知防火墙框架，在用户与目标模型之间插入独立监督模型，将安全评估分解为四个分类门控：(1)意图门——识别请求的操作目标；(2)零信任上下文门——将声称的角色和权限视为未验证证据；(3)一致性门——检测跨轮次的升级和分解；(4)输出风险门——在发布前检查候选响应。门控决策通过升级机制组合，任何可信的危险信号即可阻断交互，同时保留可审计的推理记录。

Result: 在四个越狱基准测试和一个良性安全测试集上，认知防火墙显著降低了单轮、多轮、基于权限和人工制作的攻击成功率。在三个攻击集上降至2%或以下，在最困难的人工制作集上降至14%，同时保持8%的过度拒绝率。

Conclusion: 分解式、对话级别的监督可以改进LLM安全的主动遏制和可审计性。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) can be induced to produce harmful content through multi turn strategies in which no single user message appears clearly unsafe. Existing runtime safeguards commonly evaluate prompts or responses as isolated messages, which limits their ability to recover ac-cumulated intent, verify asserted authority, or detect harmful objectives decomposed across a dialogue. This paper presents the Cognitive Firewall, a proactive runtime oversight framework that interposes an independent oversight model between a user and a protected target mod l. The framework decomposes safety assessment into four categorical gates: an intent gate that identi-fies the operational objective of a request, a zero trust context gate that treats claimed roles and permissions as unverified evidence, a consistency gate that detects escalation and decomposition across turns, and an output risk gate that inspects candidate responses before release. Gate decisions are combined through escalation rather than score averaging, allowing any confident danger signal to block an interaction while preserving an auditable rationale. Experiments on four jailbreak benchmarks and a benign safety test set show that the Cognitive Firewall substantially reduces attack success across single turn, multi turn, authority based, and human crafted attacks. It lowers attack success to 2 percent or below on three attack sets and to 14 percent on the most difficult human crafted set, while maintaining an 8 percent over refusal rate. These results indicate that decomposed, conversation level oversight can improve proactive containment and auditability for LLM safety.

</details>


### [202] [Generative AI and Federated Learning for Intrusion Detection Systems: A Survey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01305)
*Jiefei Liu,Abu Saleh Md Tayeen,Pratyay Kumar,Qixu Gong,Wenbin Jiang,Huiping Cao,Satyajayant Misra,Jayashree Harikumar*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了生成式AI与联邦学习在入侵检测系统中的应用，涵盖生成模型（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM）和FL分布式训练技术，并讨论了合成数据质量、非IID分布、通信效率等开放挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 入侵检测系统面临攻击行为演化、数据集获取困难、类别不平衡、隐私约束等挑战，而生成式AI和联邦学习为解决这些问题提供了新的技术路径。

Method: 采用结构化综述方法，首先总结代表性IDS研究方向（对抗机器学习、异常检测、IoT-IDS、可解释IDS、基准数据集），然后按模型族和任务目标分类生成式AI应用（自编码器、GAN、扩散模型、LLM），最后综述生成式AI与FL-IDS的集成研究。

Result: 梳理了生成式AI在IDS中的多种应用场景（异常检测、合成流量生成、数据增强、数据插补、对抗流量生成、警报解释），以及FL在分布式隐私保护训练中的适用性；识别了合成数据质量、真实流量生成、对抗风险、非IID分布、通信效率、基准测试、领域LLM等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 生成式AI和联邦学习为入侵检测提供了有前景的技术方向，但合成数据的真实性与质量、联邦环境下的非IID问题、以及专用网络安全LLM的构建等仍是亟待解决的关键挑战。

Abstract: Intrusion Detection Systems (IDSs) are essential for monitoring network traffic and identifying malicious activities in modern cyber-physical, Internet of Things (IoT), enterprise, and distributed network environments. However, developing reliable IDS models remains challenging because attack behaviors evolve over time, realistic datasets are difficult to obtain, traffic records may be incomplete, attack classes are often imbalanced, and privacy constraints limit centralized data collection. Recent advances in generative artificial intelligence (AI) and Federated Learning (FL) provide new opportunities to address these limitations. Generative models can support anomaly detection, synthetic traffic generation, data augmentation, data imputation, adversarial traffic generation, and IDS alert explanation. FL enables distributed IDS training without directly sharing local network traffic, making it suitable for privacy-sensitive and geographically distributed environments. This survey provides a structured review of generative AI and FL techniques for IDS. We first summarize representative IDS research directions, including adversarial machine learning, anomaly-based detection, IoT-oriented IDS, explainable IDS, and benchmark datasets. We then categorize generative AI applications in IDS according to model families and task objectives, covering autoencoder-based models, Generative Adversarial Networks (GANs), diffusion models, and Large Language Models (LLMs). Finally, we review emerging studies that integrate generative AI with FL-based IDS and discuss open challenges, including synthetic data quality, realistic traffic generation, dual-use adversarial risks, non-IID client distributions, communication-efficient model sharing, federated IDS benchmarking, and domain-specific LLMs for network security.

</details>


### [203] [An alternative approach towards attacks against fully-split PLWE instances](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01340)
*Iván Blanco-Chacón,Rodrigo Martín Sánchez-Ledesma,Raúl Durán Díaz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于根的密码攻击的泛化问题，证明在完全分裂环设置下，通过构造显式同构无法产生新的漏洞，且任何此类同构都会使样本失真而无法用于区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者旨在回答关于其先前工作中提出的基于根的攻击能否扩展到更一般情形的问题，特别是验证通过构造显式同构是否能发现新的安全漏洞。

Method: 首先构造了完全分裂多项式环与已有攻击适用多项式环之间的显式同构；然后证明应用该同构会扭曲样本分布，使结果样本无法用于区分攻击；最后证明任何完全分裂多项式环之间的同构都必须具有所构造的形式。

Result: 证明了在完全分裂设定下，通过构造显式同构的方法无法产生新的漏洞；所有可能的同构都会导致样本失真，从而无法用于区分性攻击。

Conclusion: 基于根的攻击在完全分裂多项式环上的泛化路径被封锁，该设置下的密码方案不受此类扩展攻击的威胁。

Abstract: In the present work we address some key questions regarding the generalization of root-based attacks presented in a recent work by the authors. In particular, we analyze potential root-based attacks extensions via the construction of explicit isomorphisms from vulnerable instances, and provide a formal proof that this approach will not yield any new vulnerabilities under a fully-split setting. To do so, we first construct an explicit isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings and polynomial rings where previous attacks apply and show that the application of such an isomorphism will always distort the samples in a way that the resulting samples cannot be used to distinguish. Then, we prove that any isomorphism between fully-split polynomial rings must be of the form of the constructed isomorphism.

</details>


### [204] [VeriChat: An Agentic Conversational AI Assistant for Hardware Security Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01668)
*Dipayan Saha,Khan Thamid Hasan,Shams Tarek,Sujan Kumar Saha,Mark Tehranipoor,Farimah Farahmandi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VeriChat是一个面向硬件安全验证领域的专业对话助手，采用检索增强的多智能体架构，通过三个专门智能体协同工作减少幻觉，并集成了开源EDA工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys）实现RTL设计的语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式验证。在评估中达到87.73%的Faithfulness分数，显著优于主流闭源模型，并通过AES S-Box IP的硬件木马检测案例展示了其自主发现、仿真和形式证明隐蔽密钥泄露漏洞的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 硬件安全验证是一个多阶段复杂过程，工程师需要处理设计分析、威胁考虑和验证策略，但当前验证环境缺乏结构化的安全指导支持。通用聊天机器人（如ChatGPT、Gemini）存在幻觉和静态过时知识的问题，无法提供可靠的安全验证辅助。

Method: VeriChat采用检索增强的多智能体工作流，包含三个专门智能体协同工作以减少幻觉、提高响应透明度和可靠性。同时集成开源EDA工具（Icarus Verilog、Yosys、SymbiYosys），直接在用户提供的RTL设计上执行语法检查、综合分析、仿真和形式验证。

Result: VeriChat在评估中达到87.73%的Faithfulness分数，显著优于主流闭源模型。在AES S-Box IP的硬件木马检测案例研究中，VeriChat能够自主识别、仿真并通过多轮对话工作流形式证明隐蔽的密钥泄漏漏洞。

Conclusion: VeriChat展示了领域专业化对话助手在硬件安全验证中的有效性，通过多智能体协同和EDA工具集成，既能提供可靠的上下文感知安全指导，又能执行实际的验证任务，优于通用大语言模型在该领域的表现。

Abstract: Hardware security verification is a multi-stage process in which engineers must navigate complex design analyses, threat considerations, and verification strategies. They often need security-focused guidance, yet current verification environments provide little structured support for such assistance. Although conversational AI could offer such on-demand assistance, directly using general-purpose chatbots like ChatGPT or Gemini is risky due to their tendency to hallucinate and their reliance on static, outdated knowledge. We present VeriChat, a domain-specialized conversational assistant designed to support, rather than replace, existing verification workflows by providing context-aware security guidance. VeriChat employs a retrieval-augmented, multi-agent workflow in which three specialized agents collaboratively minimize hallucinations while improving the transparency and reliability of the response. Beyond question answering, VeriChat integrates open-source EDA tools, including Icarus Verilog, Yosys, and SymbiYosys, to perform syntax checking, synthesis analysis, simulation, and formal verification directly on user-provided RTL designs. Evaluated using a comprehensive methodology, VeriChat achieves a Faithfulness score of 87.73%, significantly outperforming the leading proprietary models. We demonstrate the framework through a hardware Trojan detection case study on an AES S-Box IP, where VeriChat autonomously identifies, simulates, and formally proves a covert key-leakage vulnerability through a multi-turn conversational workflow.

</details>


### [205] [Beyond Gradient-Based Attacks: Adversarial Robustness and Explainability Stability in Cybersecurity Classifiers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01679)
*Mona Rajhans,Vishal Khawarey*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文针对对抗性攻击下网络安全分类器的可解释性稳定性问题，提出了解释稳定性指数(ESI)指标，并在四种表格安全数据集上对随机森林和XGBoost进行了评估。关键发现是：基于梯度的黑盒攻击(ZOO)在XGBoost上产生退化结果(鲁棒性指数~0.98)，而基于得分的Square攻击则揭示真实脆弱性(RI~0.36)；预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是两个独立的评估维度，需要联合测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗性攻击不仅降低网络安全分类器的预测性能，还会破坏安全分析师依赖的SHAP解释的稳定性，威胁告警分诊和理解的可靠性。现有研究缺乏对树模型(随机森林、XGBoost)在表格安全数据上解释稳定性的系统评估，也缺少量化解释漂移的统一指标。

Method: 扩展先前MLP研究至随机森林和XGBoost，使用四种表格安全数据集(钓鱼URL、UNSW-NB15、NF-ToN-IoT、HIKARI-2021)，评估五种对抗攻击方法(含三种适用于不可微树模型的黑盒方法)。提出解释稳定性指数(ESI)，基于TreeSHAP归因漂移计算，采用与鲁棒性指数(RI)相同的[0,1]度量尺度。引入双轴框架(梯度依赖性和查询效率)解释攻击排序。

Result: 梯度基黑盒攻击ZOO在XGBoost上产生退化结果(RI~0.98)因分段常数预测曲面；Score-based Square攻击揭示真实脆弱性(RI~0.36)。XGBoost的ESI为0.06-0.16(尽管ZOO鲁棒性近乎完美)，RF为0.14-0.29。预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性存在显著分离。解释了PGD在z-score归一化表格数据上的反直觉异常现象。

Conclusion: 预测鲁棒性与解释稳定性是独立维度，需要联合评估。梯度依赖性与查询效率的双轴框架为树模型集成评估提供实用指导。XGBoost的分段常数预测表面对梯度基攻击产生防御性假象，但解释稳定性仍易受攻击影响。

Abstract: Adversarial attacks on cybersecurity classifiers pose a dual threat: degrading predictions and destabilising the SHAP-based explanations that security analysts rely on to understand and triage alerts. We extend our prior MLP conference study to Random Forest and XGBoost across four tabular security datasets (phishing URLs, UNSW-NB15, NF-ToN-IoT, HIKARI-2021), evaluating five attacks including three black-box methods applicable to non-differentiable tree models. We introduce the Explainability Stability Index (ESI), a scalar metric computed from TreeSHAP attribution drift under adversarial perturbation, reported on the same [0,1] scale as the Robustness Index (RI). A key finding is that gradient-based black-box attacks (ZOO) produce degenerate results against XGBoost (apparent RI ~0.98) due to piecewise-constant prediction surfaces, while score-based Square Attack reveals genuine vulnerability (RI ~0.36). These degenerate perturbations still drive substantial attribution drift: XGBoost ESI ~0.06-0.16 despite near-perfect ZOO robustness, versus 0.14-0.29 for RF, showing that prediction robustness and explanation stability are distinct axes requiring joint measurement. A two-axis framework (gradient dependence, query efficiency) explains the observed attack ranking and yields practical guidance for tree ensemble evaluation. A step-size ablation explains a counterintuitive PGD anomaly on z-score normalised tabular data.

</details>


### [206] [Pmeta-TLA: Backdoor Attacks for Speech Classification Models via Meta-Learning with Timbre Leakage Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01702)
*Yueming Huang,Wenhan Yao,Fen Xiao,Xiarun Chen,Weiping Wen*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA)的新型后门攻击方法，利用音色信息在深度自监督特征中传播，并引入Pmeta-TLA机制实现多后门一次性嵌入，在关键词识别任务中表现出更高的攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性和更低的攻击成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究表明后门攻击对语音分类模型构成严重安全威胁，但当前语音触发器容易被深度神经网络防御器检测到，亟需探索更多潜在攻击技术以揭示和防御此类风险。

Method: 提出TLA攻击，在帧级别将音色信息传播到深度自监督特征中，产生自然感知的毒化样本；同时提出Pmeta-TLA训练机制，利用元学习和Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad)实现多后门一次性嵌入，并将TLA作为多目标攻击工具集成其中。

Result: 在关键词识别任务上，使用多种深度神经网络模型进行数据投毒后门攻击实验，结果表明所提方法相较于基线方法在攻击效能、隐蔽性、鲁棒性方面表现更优，且攻击成本更低。

Conclusion: TLA和Pmeta-TLA为语音后门攻击提供了新的有效途径，在攻击效果和隐蔽性上优于现有方法，有助于推动语音模型安全防御研究。

Abstract: Recently, speech classification methods have gained widespread adoption in intelligent gadgets. Current study indicates that backdoor attacks provide a substantial security concern to these models, underscoring the pressing necessity to investigate additional potential attack techniques to expose and prevent such risks. This work discusses the vulnerability of current speech triggers to detection by deep neural network defenders and introduces the Timbre Leakage Attack (TLA). The suggested trigger disseminates timbre information at the frame level within the deep self-supervised features, producing poisoned samples that appear natural to human perception. Furthermore, we introduce Pmeta-TLA, an innovative training mechanism for embedding numerous backdoors one time. This method proposes a multi-backdoor injection training strategy using meta-learning and Projected Conflicting Gradients (PCGrad) and introduces TLA as a multi-target attack tool within it. We performed tests on data-poisoning backdoor attacks in keyword spotting tasks utilizing some deep neural network models. Experimental results indicate that the proposed strategy attains superior Attack efficacy, enhanced stealthiness, robustness, and a reduced attack cost relative to baseline methods.

</details>


### [207] [Trust Boundary Semantic Gaps: A Multi-dimensional Analysis and Mitigation for Security-by-Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01711)
*Doyeon Kim,Jin-Young Choi,Junghee Lee*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出"信任边界语义鸿沟"（TBSG）概念，指工件的语法验证通过但无法满足接收域语义安全要求的问题。基于75起安全事件的四维分析模型（MDTBSG），提出TBSAM设计时框架来识别、优先级排序和缓解TBSG，并通过SolarWinds案例验证有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有格式、协议和签名机制仅验证工件的语法合规性（格式良好、协议合规、认证正确），但无法保证工件满足接收域所需的语义安全属性，导致通过语法验证的工件仍可能引发安全事件。这种"语法通过但语义不足"的鸿沟尚未被系统研究。

Method: 1) 对2014-2025年间75起公开报告的安全事件进行边界级分析，归纳出四维语义对齐分析模型MDTBSG（身份、空间、时间、解释）；2) 提出TBSAM设计时框架，从设计规范识别TBSG、优先级排序、追溯传播缺口至原始边界、映射到候选架构控制措施。

Result: 通过对SolarWinds/SUNBURST供应链攻击的回顾性重建应用TBSAM，展示了如何显式化接收域假设、区分本地起源缺口与传播缺口，并识别出能够中断攻击路径的控制措施。

Conclusion: 语法验证在信任边界上虽必要但不充分，将信任边界假设显式化可以补充Security-by-Design方法。

Abstract: Modern systems use format-, protocol-, and signature-based mechanisms before accepting artifacts across trust boundaries. These mechanisms are necessary: they show that an artifact is well formed, protocol-compliant, or properly authenticated. They do not, however, show that the artifact satisfies the semantic security properties required by the receiving domain. A signed update or an authenticated token may therefore be accepted yet enable compromise. We call this condition a Trust Boundary Semantic Gap (TBSG): an artifact crosses a trust boundary and passes correctly implemented syntactic validation, but the assertions established by that pass are insufficient to satisfy the receiving domain's security requirements. TBSG concerns what remains unestablished after a syntactic pass, not absent checks or implementation bugs. Analyzing 75 publicly reported security incidents (2014-2025) at the boundary level, we organize semantic misalignment into a four-dimensional analysis model: Identity, Spatial, Temporal, and Interpretation (MDTBSG). Building on it, we develop Trust Boundary Semantic Analysis and Mitigation (TBSAM), a design-time framework that identifies TBSGs from design specifications, prioritizes them, traces propagated gaps to their originating boundary, and maps each to candidate architectural controls. We apply TBSAM to a retrospective reconstruction of the SolarWinds/SUNBURST supply-chain attack, showing how it makes receiving-domain assumptions explicit, separates locally originating from propagated gaps, and identifies controls that interrupt the path. These results suggest that syntactic validation, while necessary, is not sufficient at trust boundaries, and that making trust-boundary assumptions explicit can complement Security-by-Design.

</details>


### [208] [Resilient Liquid Democracy: Mitigating Voting Power Imbalances via Secure Delegation Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01730)
*Zhuolun Li,Evangelos Pournaras*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于密封委托的液态民主机制，利用去中心化定时释放加密隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，并通过排名多重委托和个人回退选票应对代表失能。在四个真实数据集上的实验表明，委托对代表性的改善遵循可恢复缺口定律，密封形成的优势主要体现在降低权力集中度，而排名多重委托能显著减少投票损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有液态民主部署通常依赖透明委托，导致从众行为、可验证胁迫以及系统脆弱性（高支持度代表弃权时）。本文旨在解决知情专长路由与系统鲁棒性之间的张力。

Method: 采用去中心化定时释放加密实现密封委托，在形成阶段隐藏委托选择以防止从众和胁迫，最终计票时恢复公开可审计性。扩展协议引入排名多重委托和个人回退选票以应对代表失能。形式化证明了揭示前保密性和重新提交收据无关性。

Result: 在四个真实数据集（市政参与式预算选举及校准调查、20个参与式预算选举、60,000名美国选民）上的实验表明：委托是否改善代表性遵循可恢复缺口定律（仅在弃权规模大且系统性不具代表性时有帮助，否则中性或有害）；密封形成的主要优势是结构性降低权力集中度；排名多重委托与个人回退选票能显著减少投票损失，该结果在全部20个选举中复现。

Conclusion: 密封委托机制能有效缓解透明委托的缺陷，排名多重委托显著增强系统鲁棒性，且委托的效果受可恢复缺口定律约束，代表性风格委托比能力精英委托更安全。

Abstract: Liquid democracy promises to improve collective decision-making by allowing voters to vote directly, delegate their voting power to trusted participants, or combine both approaches through fallback mechanisms. However, existing deployments typically rely on transparent delegation, which exposes voters to popularity-driven herding, makes coercion verifiable, and introduces systemic fragility when highly-backed delegates abstain. In this paper, we propose a secure liquid democracy mechanism that resolves the tension between informed expertise routing and systemic robustness. We introduce a sealed delegation regime using decentralized timed-release encryption, which cryptographically hides delegation choices during the formation phase to prevent herding and coercion, while restoring full public auditability for the final tally. To address delegate failures, we extend the protocol with ranked multi-delegation and personal fallback ballots. We formally prove pre-reveal secrecy and resubmission receipt-freeness for our protocol. Finally, we evaluate the mechanism on four real datasets, a municipal participatory-budgeting election with a calibration survey, twenty further participatory-budgeting elections, and 60,000 US voters with an objective competence measure. They show that whether delegation improves representational accuracy follows a recoverable-gap law; it helps only when abstention is large and systematically unrepresentative, and is otherwise neutral or harmful, with representative-style delegation safer than delegating to a competence elite. The benefit of sealed formation is primarily structural, sharply reducing power concentration rather than directly improving accuracy; and ranked multi-delegation with personal fallback ballots sharply reduces vote loss under realistic and targeted delegate failures, a result that replicates across all twenty elections.

</details>


### [209] [Knowledge Over Parameters: Evolving Smart Contract Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01742)
*Yuqiang Sun,Han Liu,Ying Li,Yiran Zhang,Zong Cao,Ziyun Guo,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: EvoVuln 是一种自动化框架，将智能合约漏洞检测重构为程序性知识演化问题，通过运行时IoC架构和两阶段演化流水线，仅需极少标注样本即可合成并优化检测逻辑，在五种真实漏洞类型上达到71%宏平均F1分数，超越所有基线，且演化知识可跨模型迁移，单次演化成本低于50美元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的智能合约漏洞检测方法存在瓶颈：基于提示的方法依赖人工设计检测规则，而微调方法需要大量标注数据——这在漏洞检测领域本身稀缺。因此，需要一种能自动合成和优化检测逻辑、且仅需极少标注样本的方法。

Method: 提出EvoVuln框架，包含两个核心机制：(1) 运行时IoC架构，将检测规则编译为可执行策略，严格解耦确定性控制流与LLM语义推理，生成密集诊断遥测以精确定位错误；(2) 两阶段演化流水线：冷启动阶段通过自动合成边界用例引导初始规则并进行压力测试，小样本演化阶段仅用每种漏洞类型5个脆弱样本和5个安全样本将策略锚定到真实语义——整个过程无需参数更新，基于溯因语义调试来优化规则。

Result: 在五种真实世界漏洞类型上，EvoVuln达到71%宏平均F1分数，优于所有基线方法；演化后的程序性知识可跨模型迁移：使轻量低成本模型超越更大的零样本模型19个百分点，且无需重新训练即可迁移到其他LLM，单次演化成本低于50美元。

Conclusion: EvoVuln证明了将漏洞检测建模为程序性知识演化问题的有效性，通过极小样本即可自动合成高质量的检测逻辑，且该知识具有良好的可迁移性和成本效益，为LLM在安全领域的实际应用提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Smart contract vulnerabilities are predominantly logic bugs whose detection requires structured, step-by-step procedural knowledge of attack patterns and contract semantics. Existing LLM-based methods struggle to generate this knowledge automatically: prompt-based methods rely on manually crafted detection rules, while fine-tuning requires massive labeled datasets that are inherently scarce in this domain. We present EvoVuln, an automated framework that reformulates vulnerability detection as a procedural knowledge evolution problem, synthesizing and refining detection logic using only a minimal number of labeled samples.
  To achieve this, EvoVuln introduces two key mechanisms. First, a Runtime with an Inversion of Control (IoC) architecture compiles detection rules into Executable Policies. This strictly decouples deterministic control flow from LLM semantic reasoning, ensuring faithful logical adherence and producing dense diagnostic telemetry for precise error localization. Second, a two-phase evolution pipeline refines the rule via abductive semantic debugging without any parameter updates: Cold Start bootstraps and stress-tests an initial rule using auto-synthesized corner cases; Few-Shot Evolving then grounds the policy in real-world semantics using only five vulnerable and five safe examples per vulnerability type.
  Evaluated across five real-world vulnerability types, EvoVuln achieves a 71% macro-average F1-score, outperforming all baselines. The evolved procedural knowledge is portable across models: it enables a lightweight, low-cost model to surpass a much larger zero-shot model by 19 percentage points, and transfers to other LLMs without retraining, at a one-time evolution cost under $50.

</details>


### [210] [Has This Checkpoint Been Abliterated? A Two-Signal Audit and Its Failure Map](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01854)
*Gabriel Hurtado*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种无需阈值的检查点审计方法，通过结合参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙和权重恢复能量两个廉价内部信号，在273个检查点注册表上实现AUROC 0.95，有效检测被剥离拒绝机制的开源权重检查点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平台在部署前无法判断开源权重检查点是否被剥离了拒绝机制，运行时防护措施只能评分生成结果而非工件本身，需要一种在部署前即可检测的方法。

Method: 结合两种廉价内部信号：参考锚定的激活拒绝间隙（提供拒绝特异性）和基座到候选权重差异的权重恢复能量（提供召回率），两者负相关且标签互补，通过z-sum进行无阈值审计。

Result: 在273个检查点注册表（涵盖Qwen、DeepSeek-distilled Qwen、Llama、Gemma）上，z-sum分离57个公开abliteration和37个良性微调/合并/指令微调，AUROC 0.95，显著优于单一信号（0.84、0.90），Youden校准阈值在保留族上达到平衡准确率0.89（FPR 0.11），仅漏检57个中的4个。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种有效的分类检查工具，而非防篡改方案；它假定有可信参考，且其声明受评估注册表的限制。同时映射了两种失败模式：伪造参考可零训练规避检测，以及白盒所有者可训练出超过阈值但仍不安全的检查点。

Abstract: Can a platform tell, before deployment, whether an open-weight checkpoint has had its refusal mechanism stripped? Runtime guards cannot: they score generations, not the artifact. We combine two cheap internal signals, a reference-anchored activation refusal-gap and a weight-recovery energy of the base-to-candidate weight difference, into a threshold-free checkpoint audit. The two are negatively correlated and label-complementary: the gap supplies refusal-specificity and the weight energy supplies recall. On a 273-checkpoint registry spanning Qwen, DeepSeek-distilled Qwen, Llama, and Gemma, their z-sum separates 57 public abliterations from 37 benign fine-tunes, merges, and instruction-tunes at AUROC 0.95, significantly above either signal alone (0.84, 0.90), and a Youden-calibrated threshold transfers to held-out families at balanced accuracy 0.89 (FPR 0.11), missing only 4 of 57. We then map two failures, in order of severity: a spoofed reference evades both axes with no training (ΔW=0, \r{ho}=1 by construction), and a white-box owner trains a checkpoint past the threshold while it stays guard-unsafe and coherent. The audit is effective triage, not tamper-proofing: it presumes an attested reference, and its claims are bounded by the registry we evaluate it on.

</details>


### [211] [Behind the Refusal: Determining Guardrail Activation via Behavioral Monitoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02121)
*William Hackett,Peter Garraghan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出首个黑盒护栏侦察方法，通过HTTP、词汇和时序信号检测AI系统中护栏的存在，区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝，实现100%检测准确率和98%的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在对抗性测试中，研究人员难以区分护栏拦截和LLM拒绝，而两者需要不同的绕过技术，影响攻击策略优化。

Method: 通过黑盒方式监控HTTP响应、词汇特征和时序信号，对恶意与良性交互的行为差异进行统计分析，检测护栏存在并识别其屏蔽的内容类别。

Result: 护栏检测准确率100%，良性与恶意交互行为分离具有统计显著性(q < 0.001)；区分护栏拦截与LLM拒绝的平均F1分数达98%。

Conclusion: 该方法能有效识别护栏存在并区分拦截类型，为AI安全评估和对抗测试提供重要工具。

Abstract: As Large Language Models (LLMs) and agentic systems become integrated into real-world applications, ensuring their safety and security is critical. Guardrail systems that detect and block malicious instructions sent to and from an LLM are an essential component of AI security. However, researchers conducting black-box adversarial emulation against production AI systems often struggle to determine whether a guardrail block or an LLM rejection has occurred. This distinction is important because the techniques used to bypass guardrails can differ substantially from those used to bypass LLM safety alignment, and has a material impact on attack technique selection and optimization. We propose the first black-box guardrail reconnaissance methodology, which detects the presence of a guardrail within a target AI system through behavioral monitoring of HTTP, lexical, and timing signals, assuming only black-box access and zero prior knowledge of the guardrail or AI system. Experiments demonstrate that our approach detects guardrail presence with 100% accuracy, with statistically significant behavioral separation between benign and malicious interactions (q < 0.001). Our approach further identifies the content categories a guardrail is designed to block, and distinguishes guardrail blocks from LLM rejection on unseen prompts with an average F1 score of 98%.

</details>


### [212] [Cloak and Detonate: Scanner Evasion and Dynamic Detection of Agent Skill Malware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02357)
*Zimo Ji,Congying Xu,Zongjie Li,Yudong Gao,Xin Wei,Shuai Wang,Shing-Chi Cheung*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文揭示了LLM编码代理技能市场中的供应链攻击面，提出SkillCloak（一种能够绕过现有静态扫描仪的规避框架）和SkillDetonate（一种基于运行时行为审计的新型防御方案），证明了外观审计的不足和运行时检测的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM编码代理依赖第三方技能市场，恶意技能可窃取凭证、外泄源码或安装后门。现有防御基于静态模式匹配或LLM-as-judge分析，但无法抵御保留恶意行为的同时改变载荷外观的适应性规避攻击。

Method: 1) SkillCloak: 载荷保留的规避框架，包含结构混淆（将可见载荷指标重写为语义等价形式）和自提取技能(SFS)打包（在安装时隐藏恶意组件，代理执行时恢复）。2) SkillDetonate: 行为中心运行时审计器，在沙箱中执行技能，通过OS边界信息流证据检测恶意效果，结合按需闭包提升和基于标记的污点分析。

Result: SFS打包绕过8个扫描仪中超90%，结构混淆绕过80%以上静态扫描仪，在混合扫描仪上达96%。SkillDetonate检测97%的攻击（2%误报率），对真实恶意技能保持87%检测率。

Conclusion: 基于外观的扫描审计不足以防御LLM代理技能供应链攻击，运行时行为审计（如SkillDetonate）是更有效的防御方向。

Abstract: LLM coding agents increasingly rely on third-party agent skills from public marketplaces, which execute with the agent's privileges and create a software supply-chain attack surface: a malicious skill can steal credentials, exfiltrate source code, or install backdoors. Existing defenses use static skill scanners based on pattern matching or LLM-as-judge analysis, but it remains unclear whether they withstand adaptive evasions that preserve malicious behavior while changing payload appearance.
  This paper first presents an adversarial study of existing skill scanners through SkillCloak, a payload-preserving evasion framework that keeps the attack semantics intact while transforming their visible form. SkillCloak uses two complementary strategies: Structural Obfuscation, which rewrites visible payload indicators into semantically equivalent forms, and Self-Extracting Skill (SFS) Packing, which hides malicious components from the install-time view and restores them during agent execution. Across eight scanners and 1,613 in-the-wild malicious skills, SFS Packing bypasses every scanner at over 90%, while Structural Obfuscation bypasses over 80% on most static scanners and reaches 96% on a hybrid scanner, showing that appearance-based auditing is insufficient.
  Motivated by this finding, we propose SkillDetonate, a behavior-centric runtime auditor that executes skills in a sandbox and detects malicious effects through OS-boundary information-flow evidence rather than install-time appearance. SkillDetonate combines on-demand closure lift, which observes instructions materialized during execution, with marker-based taint analysis, which tracks sensitive-data flows across the agent context, files, processes, and network operations. The results show that SkillDetonate detects 97% of attacks at a 2% false-positive rate and sustains 87% detection on real-world malicious skills.

</details>


### [213] [SoK: A Taxonomy for Cybersecurity Incident Response Influence Factors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02451)
*Thomas Biege,Marius Brockhoff,Jonas Kaspereit,Fabian Ising,Lea Gröber,Sebastian Schinzel*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本研究通过系统综述417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物，构建了"网络安全事件响应影响因素分类体系"(CIR-IF Taxonomy)，系统化整理了1999年至2024年中期影响组织网络安全事件响应准备与执行的因素，并与NIST等现有框架对比，证明该分类体系提供了更丰富、更严谨、更系统化的视角。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 网络安全事件响应领域的文献不断增长，但缺乏统一的框架来系统组织已有知识；事件响应涉及技术、人机交互、组织理论、人因工程等多个领域，需要一个综合整合的视角来帮助研究者识别未被充分探索的领域。

Method: 采用系统综述方法，对417篇学术文献和40篇非科学出版物进行分析，构建了CIR-IF Taxonomy分类体系；将分类结果与7个既有科学框架及NIST SP 800-61r3事件响应配置文件中的NIST网络安全框架要素进行系统比较。

Result: CIR-IF Taxonomy相比现有框架（包括NIST等）提供了更丰富、更严谨、更系统化的因素组织方式，覆盖了影响网络安全事件响应的多维因素。

Conclusion: 该分类体系为网络安全事件响应领域提供了一个统一的知识组织框架，有助于研究者系统化定位研究空白，更有效地开展实证和理论探索。

Abstract: Cybersecurity incident response has emerged as a critical area of interest for both researchers and practitioners. The corpus of literature on cybersecurity incident response is expanding, yet a unified framework for systematically organizing the accumulated knowledge remains absent. The aspects of incident response span multiple domains, including technology, human-computer interaction, organizational theory, and human factors. A comprehensive, integrative perspective on these factors can enable researchers to identify underexplored areas and more effectively target their empirical and theoretical investigations. Our study systematizes the factors that influence organizational preparedness for and response to cybersecurity incidents. Through a systematic review of academic literature (n = 417) and non-scientific publications (n = 40), we derived the "Cybersecurity Incident Response Influencing Factor Taxonomy" (\textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy}). Existing empirical findings were classified within this taxonomy, providing a comprehensive and up-to-date overview of knowledge from the period 1999 to mid-2024. The taxonomy categories were systematically compared with seven established scientific frameworks and with the \textit{NIST Cyber Security Framework} elements referenced in the \textit{NIST Special Publication 800-61r3} incident response profile. The results of this comparison show that the \textit{CIR-IF Taxonomy} delivers a richer, more rigorous, and more systematically organized view of the factors that drive and shape incident response.

</details>


### [214] [Hamm-Grams: An Algorithm for Mining Regular Expressions of Bytes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01445)
*Derek Everett,Edward Raff,James Holt*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为hamm-grams的新型鲁棒静态特征，用于恶意软件检测和分类，通过局部敏感哈希和聚类算法高效生成，相比传统n-gram特征更具鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的n-gram特征在恶意软件检测中虽然有效，但本质上是脆弱的（brittle），容易因微小的代码变种而失效，需要构建更鲁棒的特征来应对恶意软件的持续演化。

Method: 提出hamm-grams特征（固定长度带单字符通配符的正则表达式），设计了一种新的局部敏感哈希（LSH）来使小汉明距离的字符串对产生碰撞，并在哈希桶内进行聚类以放置通配符位置。

Result: 在恶意软件分类和检测任务上展示了hamm-gram特征相比传统n-gram的优势。

Conclusion: hamm-grams是一种更具鲁棒性的静态特征，能有效提升恶意软件检测系统的性能。

Abstract: Malware poses a critical and ever-evolving threat, and robust and effective systems for detecting and classifying malware are of essential importance. $n$-grams features are among the common static features used in effective machine learning systems for malware, but these features are inherently brittle. We propose an algorithm for constructing more robust features, hamm-grams, which are a special class of regular expressions having a fixed length and single-character wildcards. We devise an efficient algorithm for finding common hamm-grams using a new locality-sensitive hash designed to produce collisions among pairs of small Hamming distance and a clustering within hash buckets to place wildcards. We then demonstrate the advantages of these features in malware classification and detection tasks.

</details>


### [215] [From Forgeries to Foundation Models: A Systematic Survey of Identity Document Attack and Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01442)
*Gourab Das,Pavan Kumar C,Raghavendra Ramachandra*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文首次统一了针对身份伪造攻击的威胁模型，涵盖物理呈现、数字注入和生成式AI合成三类攻击，系统梳理了检测方法从规则到多模态大模型的演进，揭示了基准测试与现实部署之间的持续差距，并发现非拉丁文字生成中的脚本依赖不稳定性（SDGI）问题。零样本测试显示，即使在最强模型下，最安全操作条件下的APCER值仍超25%，表明跨域泛化能力存在显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具使得高保真文档伪造和字段级篡改变得极易操作，而现有检测方法所依赖的基准测试无法反映这一新型威胁，导致物理呈现、数字注入和完全生成式合成这三类攻击缺乏统一的威胁模型和评估框架。

Method: 采用统一的身份验证威胁模型对三类攻击（呈现攻击、数字注入攻击、生成式AI驱动合成）进行系统梳理；对2019-2025年的公开数据集进行系统性审计以暴露真实差距；分析多模态大模型在身份证件篡改中的表现，识别非拉丁文字修复中的脚本依赖生成不稳定性；通过零样本基准测试评估现有模型的跨域泛化能力。

Result: 发现了基准测试与部署之间的持续真实差距；识别出脚本依赖生成不稳定性（SDGI）这一非拉丁文字修复中的典型字体失败模式；零样本测试表明最强公开模型在安全导向操作条件下APCER值超过25%，跨域泛化能力严重不足。

Conclusion: 未来需要构建法证基础扎实、隐私保护完善且法律问责明确的身份验证系统。

Abstract: Identity document forgery has undergone a fundamental capability shift: generative AI tools now enable high-fidelity document synthesis and field-level manipulation with minimal technical expertise, while detection methods remain constrained by benchmarks that do not reflect this threat. The resulting attack surface spans physical presentation, digital injection, and fully generative synthesis, introducing distinct forensic failure modes that require a unified threat model and evaluation framework. This survey provides, to our knowledge, the first unified treatment of Presentation Attacks, Digital Injection Attacks, and GenAI-driven synthesis within a single identity verification threat model. We trace detection methodologies from rule-based heuristics through forensic localisation, injection-aware pipelines, foundation models, and few-shot frameworks. A systematic audit of public datasets from 2019--2025 exposes a persistent Reality Gap between benchmark conditions and operational deployment. We further analyse large multimodal models for identity document manipulation, identifying Script-Dependent Generative Instability (SDGI) as a recurring typographic failure mode in non-Latin script inpainting. Finally, zero-shot benchmarking on unseen synthesised ID cards shows that even the strongest publicly available models achieve APCER values above 25% under security-oriented operating conditions, highlighting substantial limits in cross-domain generalisation. We conclude by outlining future directions toward forensically grounded, privacy-preserving, and legally accountable identity verification systems.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [216] [eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE): Learning Data-Adaptive Gaussian Mixture Priors for Latent Distributions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01275)
*Qijun Chen,Shaofan Li*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出X-VAE，用预训练自编码器的经验均值和标准差构建数据自适应高斯先验替代标准正态先验，并引入潜在缩放因子控制生成样本的多样性与保真度平衡，在标准数据集上验证了重构质量和样本可控性的提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准VAE假设各向同性标准高斯先验与复杂数据集的真实潜在分布不匹配，导致重构精度低、样本质量差、潜在空间表达能力受限。

Method: 1) 用预训练自编码器潜在编码的经验均值与标准差参数化数据自适应高斯先验；2) 引入潜在缩放因子显式控制采样方差，平衡样本多样性与保真度；3) 推导相应KL散度目标函数。

Result: X-VAE在保持重构质量的同时，使潜在表示更贴合经验数据分布，提升了样本可控性和生成真实性。

Conclusion: 所提方法适用于工业与工程设计等对生成解有严格结构/功能约束且需探索设计空间的场景。

Abstract: Variational Autoencoders (VAEs) commonly assume a standard isotropic Gaussian prior over the latent space, an assumption that often fails to capture the true distribution of latent representations for complex datasets. This mismatch can limit reconstruction accuracy, reduce sample quality, and constrain the expressive power of the learned latent space. We propose the eXact-Prior Variational Autoencoder (X-VAE), a framework that replaces the conventional standard normal prior with a Gaussian prior derived from the latent representations of a pretrained autoencoder (AE). Specifically, the empirical mean and standard deviation of the AE latent codes are used to parameterize a data-adaptive prior that more closely reflects the underlying structure of the training data. During generation, X-VAE introduces a latent scaling factor that enables explicit control over the variance of the sampled latent vectors, providing a simple mechanism for balancing sample diversity and fidelity. This flexibility makes the proposed approach particularly well suited for applications such as industrial and engineering design, where generated solutions must satisfy strict structural or functional constraints while still permitting meaningful design exploration. We present the mathematical formulation of well-suited X-VAE, derive the corresponding KL divergence objective for the proposed prior, and evaluate the method on standard benchmark datasets. Experimental results demonstrate that X-VAE preserves reconstruction quality while producing latent representations that better align with the empirical data distribution, leading to improved controllability and more realistic generated samples.

</details>


### [217] [Full Bayesian Reinforcement Learning via LF-IBIS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01741)
*Stefano Masini,Cecilia Viscardi,Michela Baccini*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出LF-IBIS算法，一种无需似然函数的贝叶斯强化学习方法，通过结合近似贝叶斯计算与迭代批量重要性采样，实现在环境动态无法用显式似然描述的场景下的完整贝叶斯推断，并在临床试验自适应随机化等场景中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有贝叶斯强化学习方法大多需要显式的似然函数，但在许多实际场景中，环境动态的似然函数难以获得或难以处理，限制了BRL在真实世界中的应用。

Method: 将近似贝叶斯计算（ABC）与迭代批量重要性采样（IBIS）相结合，提出LF-IBIS算法，在线更新智能体信念，无需显式似然函数即可对环境和最优策略进行近似后验推断。

Result: 在临床试验自适应随机化的仿真研究中，通过与闭式后验对比验证了方法的有效性；此外在无闭式后验的场景中展示了在线策略更新的能力。

Conclusion: LF-IBIS能够在缺乏显式似然函数的复杂环境中进行完整的贝叶斯推断，为探索-利用权衡提供贝叶斯处理框架，拓展了BRL在真实场景中的适用性。

Abstract: Reinforcement Learning (RL) is a sequential decision-making framework in which an agent learns optimal policies through interaction with an environment by maximizing cumulative rewards. Among RL methods, Bayesian Reinforcement Learning (BRL) addresses common practical challenges related to data scarcity by leveraging prior knowledge about the environment and sequential belief updates. However, most BRL approaches require an explicit likelihood function, which is frequently inaccessible or intractable in real-world settings.
  We propose Likelihood-Free Iterated Batch Importance Sampling (LF-IBIS), a novel algorithm for BRL that updates the agent's beliefs online as new interactions become available. By combining Approximate Bayesian Computation with Iterated Batch Importance Sampling, LF-IBIS enables full Bayesian inference in settings where the environment dynamics are not described by an explicit or tractable likelihood. The method yields approximate posterior distributions over both environment parameters and optimal policies, providing a quantification of policy uncertainty useful for a Bayesian treatment of the exploration-exploitation trade-off. We test the method on a simulation study in response-adaptive randomization in clinical trials, where closed-form posteriors enable validation. Additional experiments address settings where the posterior has no closed form and illustrate online policy updating based on the posterior distribution of the optimal policy.

</details>


### [218] [Statistical Properties of $k$-means Clustering for Data Missing Completely at Random](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01945)
*Xin Guan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了缺失数据下k-means聚类的统计性质，建立了基于缺失数据的聚类中心估计的相合性与渐近正态性理论，给出了估计收敛到真实聚类中心的充分条件，并揭示了MCAR机制下高维应用面临的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的k-means聚类无法直接处理不完整数据，现有的基于k-means的缺失数据聚类方法大多侧重于提升实际聚类精度，但缺乏渐近意义上的理论保证。

Method: 首先建立√n-过剩风险界并证明一般缺失机制下聚类中心估计的相合性；对MCAR机制进一步推导√n-收敛速率和渐近正态性；给出缺失概率与真实簇间分离度相关的充分条件，确保缺失数据估计的簇中心收敛到真实簇中心。

Result: 在MCAR机制下，要达到√n-速率收敛且收敛到真实簇中心，需要k个真实中心在每个维度上均互不相同，这揭示了高维应用中面临的显著挑战。数值模拟验证了理论分析结果。

Conclusion: 本文为缺失数据下的k-means聚类提供了系统的理论保证，阐明了其统计性质与适用条件。

Abstract: The classical $k$-means clustering cannot be directly used to incomplete data, and existing $k$-means-based clustering for missing data primarily focus on improving the practical accuracy of clustering, whereas most of them lack theoretical guarantees in the asymptotic sense. In this paper, we investigate the statistical properties of $k$-means clustering in the presence of missing data. We first establish the $\sqrt{n}$-excess risk bound and prove the consistency of the estimated cluster centers under general missing mechanisms. For the Missing Completely at Random (MCAR) mechanism, we further derive the $\sqrt{n}$-convergence rate and asymptotic normality of the estimated cluster centers. Moreover, we study in what cases the cluster centers estimated by incomplete data converge to the true cluster centers of original fully observed data, and give a sufficient condition about the missing probability and the separation among true clusters. These results provide a theoretical guarantee for missing-data-$k$-means. Notably, our analysis reveal that under MCAR mechanism, both achieving the $\sqrt{n}$-rate and converging to the true cluster centers require $k$ true centers to be distinct in every dimension, highlighting the significant challenges of application in high-dimensional regimes. Finally, we conduct numerical simulations on synthetic incomplete datasets to support our theoretical analysis results.

</details>


### [219] [Autorelevance function and other feature relevance measures for univariate time series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01959)
*Julian Cardenas,Jamie Arjona,Pedro Delicado*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种模型无关的方法来衡量单变量时间序列预测模型中滞后的相关性，结合Ghost变量、Shapley值和加性重要性度量，引入了自相关和偏自相关函数作为滞后重要性值，并提出了一种新的方法替代基于联盟的方法中缺失的特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的时间序列模型缺乏一种模型无关的方法来量化不同滞后对预测的重要性，特别是如何将特征重要性方法（如Shapley值）有效应用于时间序列中的滞后结构分析。

Method: 结合Ghost变量和Shapley值的框架，提出auto-relevance和partial auto-relevance函数作为滞后重要性度量；提出用模型的一步预测替代联盟方法中缺失特征的新方法。

Result: 在模拟和真实数据案例中，使用SARMA和RNN模型验证，计算出的相关性度量在几乎所有情况下成功展示了预期的滞后结构。

Conclusion: 该组合框架视角特别适用于时间序列分析，能够有效揭示模型中的滞后重要性结构。

Abstract: We propose a model agnostic methodology to measure lag relevance in machine learning forecasting models applied to univariate time series. Particularly, we are working in the context of time series using the frameworks of Ghost variables and Shapley values, together with additive importance measures, to introduce the auto-relevance and partial auto-relevance functions as the lag importance values. Additionally, we propose a novel method to replace absent features in coalition based methods with a one step forecast from the same model. We evaluate these proposals under different simulations and real data cases. This combined framework perspective is particularly suitable for time series. In addition, to show our discoveries we use a pull of models from the seasonal ARMA family and recurrent neural networks. We found that the calculated relevance measures successfully demonstrate the expected lag structure in almost all cases.

</details>


### [220] [Born Discrete, Made Smooth: Variational Formulation of Shallow Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02003)
*Matej Benko,Pierre Bousquet,Iwona Chlebicka,Błażej Miasojedow*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出将浅层神经网络的离散训练问题替换为连续变分代理，在加权Sobolev空间上定义λ-凸泛函族，证明全局适定性、稳定性及几乎C^3正则性。最优参数密度可通过求解单个线性系统获得，无需迭代优化，并建立了显式的泛化误差控制和有限宽度网络的收敛速率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经网络的优化原理在理论上难以解释，通常面临非凸景观和随机启发式方法的局限。现有Wasserstein或平均场方法存在正则性不足和离散化挑战，亟需一种更严谨的变分框架来理解神经网络的训练和泛化。

Method: 将离散训练问题替换为连续变分代理，在加权Sobolev空间中定义λ-凸泛函，利用椭圆正则性和凸分析，将最优参数密度问题转化为求解单个线性系统。

Result: 证明了变分问题的全局适定性、稳定性和几乎C^3正则性；建立了1/α量级的泛化误差控制和有限宽度网络O(1/N)的收敛速率；桥接了NTK和特征学习机制。

Conclusion: 该变分框架为理解过度参数化提供了原则性工具，通过变分微积分视角统一了NTK和特征学习范式，展示了连续优化方法在神经网络理论中的潜力。

Abstract: Although neural networks are remarkably effective, their underlying optimization principles remain theoretically elusive, often characterized by non-convex landscapes and stochastic heuristics. In this work, we propose a paradigm shift by replacing the discrete training problem of shallow neural networks with a well-posed continuum variational surrogate. We identify a family of $λ$-convex functionals over parameter densities in weighted Sobolev spaces and prove that these variational problems are globally well-posed, stable, and exhibit unexpected almost $C^3$ regularity.
  Unlike existing Wasserstein-based or Mean-Field approaches, which often face limited regularity and discretization challenges, our formulation provides direct access to elliptic regularity and convex analysis. This allows us to prove that the optimal parameter density can be obtained by solving a single linear system, bypassing iterative optimization entirely. We establish explicit generalization error controls at a rate of $1/α$ relative to the regularization parameter, and prove that finite-width networks of size $N$ achieve the continuum optimum at an $O(1/N)$ rate. This perspective bridges the gap between the Neural Tangent Kernel (NTK) and feature-learning regimes, providing a principled framework for understanding over-parameterization through the lens of variational calculus.

</details>


### [221] [Prediction Sets for Counterfactual Decisions: Coverage, Optimality, and Conformal Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02206)
*Yurui Zheng,Ying Jin*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出一个决策理论框架，用于不确定性信息下的反事实决策。核心概念是"策略耦合覆盖"——即预测集诱导的行动下实现结果的覆盖——作为不确定性与行动之间的最优无损失接口。作者证明了该框架的极小极大最优性、与通用覆盖和风险厌恶优化的等价性，并提出了两阶段程序PC-RACP，在保证覆盖的同时提升效用。模拟和邮件营销实验验证了方法有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有不确定性量化方法（如共形预测）虽能提供统计覆盖保证，但统计有效性本身并不能直接指导决策或保证决策最优性。特别是在反事实场景中，实际结果取决于所采取的行动，不确定性无法独立于决策规则进行指定，存在关键的理论空白。

Method: 1) 提出"策略耦合覆盖"概念，即预测集诱导的行动下实际结果的覆盖概率；2) 论证基于该概念的最大-最小决策规则在分布模糊性下是极小极大最优的；3) 证明策略耦合覆盖下的优化等价于通用覆盖优化和直接风险厌恶策略优化；4) 提出两阶段程序PC-RACP，用有限样本保证近似最优预测集。

Result: PC-RACP在保持有效覆盖的同时比现有方法获得更高效用；忽略决策问题的反事实结构在有效性和效用上都是次优的。

Conclusion: 策略耦合覆盖是反事实决策中连接不确定性与行动的最优接口，基于此设计的PC-RACP方法在理论和实践上都优于忽略反事实结构的传统方法。

Abstract: Predictions are increasingly used to guide high-stakes decisions, from treatment selection to policy making. To ensure reliability with imperfect predictions, uncertainty quantification methods such as conformal prediction build prediction sets with coverage guarantees. However, statistical validity alone does not immediately determine the decisions to take, nor the optimality thereof. This gap is especially delicate in counterfactual settings where the outcome that materializes depends on the action taken, so uncertainty cannot be specified independently of the decision rule.
  We develop a decision-theoretic framework for uncertainty-informed counterfactual decisions. We identify a novel notion of \emph{policy-coupled coverage} -- namely, coverage of the realized outcome under the action induced by the prediction sets themselves -- as the optimal and lossless interface between uncertainty and action. It plays three roles. First, it justifies acting via a natural max-min rule as minimax-optimal under distributional ambiguity. Second, optimizing prediction sets under policy-coupled coverage is equivalent both to a stronger universal-coverage formulation and to the direct risk-averse optimization over policies and utility certificates; this equivalence yields the explicit form of the population-optimal prediction sets. Third, it admits a two-stage procedure, Policy-Coupled Risk-Averse Conformal Prediction (PC-RACP), that approximates these optimal sets with rigorous finite-sample coverage. Simulations and a real email-marketing experiment confirm that PC-RACP delivers higher utility than existing approaches while maintaining valid coverage, and that ignoring the counterfactual structure of the decision problem is suboptimal for both validity and utility.

</details>


### [222] [An Additive MLP-GNN Framework for Characterizing Chemical and Structural Contributions to Aqueous Solubility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02212)
*Sampreeti Bhattacharya,Arkaprava Roy*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 提出了一种可加性深度学习框架，将物理化学描述符和分子图拓扑信息分离处理，分别通过MLP（化学分支）和GNN（结构分支）编码，仅在预测阶段结合，实现溶解度预测中化学与结构贡献的透明分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有溶解度预测模型将物理化学描述符与分子图信息合并为单一表示，导致无法区分预测是由全局化学性质驱动还是由分子结构驱动，缺乏可解释性。

Method: 构建双分支可加性框架：化学分支用MLP编码物理化学描述符，结构分支用GNN编码分子图拓扑，两分支输出在预测阶段通过加性模型（可选乘性交互）组合。采用AqSolDB预训练+BigSolDB2微调策略，并通过线性投影、嵌入摘要和GNNExplainer掩码进行解释性分析。

Result: 在两个数据集上取得具有竞争力的预测性能；预训练+微调策略显著提升精度并降低运行间波动；解释性分析表明化学分支对齐经典物化描述符，结构分支捕捉图拓扑和官能团模式。

Conclusion: 该框架在保持良好预测性能的同时，使化学和结构信息各自的作用更加透明，提升了模型的可解释性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Aqueous solubility is a key property in early-stage drug discovery, but most predictive models merge physicochemical descriptors and molecular graph information into a single representation, obscuring whether a prediction is driven by global chemistry, molecular structure, or both. We present an additive deep-learning framework that keeps these two sources of information separate throughout training: physicochemical descriptors are encoded by a multilayer perceptron (the chemical branch) and molecular graph topology by a graph neural network (the structural branch), with the two outputs combined only at the prediction stage through an additive model with an optional multiplicative interaction. This design provides a direct decomposition of chemical and structural components that can be examined separately after training. Furthermore, pretraining on the larger AqSolDB dataset and fine-tuning on the smaller BigSolDB2 dataset substantially improve accuracy and reduce run-to-run variations, indicating generalizability of the learned features from the data-rich settings. We further interpret the fitted model using best linear projections of the branch outputs, molecule-level embedding summaries across solubility classes, and atom-level GNNExplainer masks aggregated over functional groups. These analyses show that the chemical branch aligns with familiar physicochemical descriptors, while the structural branch captures graph-topological and functional-group patterns associated with solubility. Across both datasets, the framework attains competitive predictive performance while making the distinct roles of chemical and structural information more transparent.

</details>


### [223] [The Dual Nature of LLM Persona: Aggregated Tendencies and Frame-Dependent Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02368)
*Yuan Yuan*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 通过构建IPIP-50响应内的相关矩阵并分析SPD流形几何结构，发现LLM人格表达包含两个可分离的成分：聚合特征（大五人格得分）对框架鲁棒但受随机化影响（下降21%），几何特征（SPD流形）在框架错位时崩溃（下降42%）但在共享框架下可恢复至84%，表明人格几何结构不是内在的而是框架依赖的协调模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM人格评估通过心理测量问卷时通常依赖聚合分数，忽略了实例内相关结构（几何结构），本文旨在测试该几何结构是内在属性还是框架依赖的。

Method: 从IPIP-50响应中构建实例内相关矩阵，在SPD流形上分析几何结构，通过操纵GPT-4o模拟美国人和华裔美国人角色时的题目顺序进行实验。

Result: 聚合特征（大五得分）在随机化下下降21%但对框架鲁棒；几何特征（SPD流形）在框架错位时下降42%，但在共享框架下恢复至84%，超过聚合特征的76%。

Conclusion: LLM人格几何结构不是内在特质，而是框架依赖的协调模式，编码了聚合信息无法捕捉的信息，需要建立双重性质框架（框架依赖的几何 vs 框架鲁棒的聚合），挑战了静态特质观念。

Abstract: Evaluations of LLM personas via psychometric questionnaires typically rely on aggregate scores, discarding within-instance correlation structure. We test whether this geometric structure is intrinsic or frame-dependent. Constructing within-instance correlation matrices from IPIP-50 responses, we analyze geometry on SPD manifolds under manipulated question orderings in GPT-4o simulating American and Chinese-American personas. We find that persona expression comprises two dissociable components: aggregated features (Big Five scores) degrade under randomization (21% drop) but are frame-robust; geometric features (SPD manifold) collapse under frame misalignment (42% drop) but recover substantially (to 84%) under shared frames, surpassing aggregated features (76%). This collapse-recovery pattern reveals that persona geometry is not intrinsic but a frame-dependent coordination pattern encoding information invisible to aggregation. Our findings establish a dual-nature framework for LLM personas, frame-dependent geometry versus frame-robust aggregates, necessitating frame-aware evaluation and challenging static trait conceptions.

</details>


<div id='math.ST'></div>

# math.ST [[Back]](#toc)

### [224] [Beyond Degree: Rooted Motif Signatures for Latent Position Identifiability in Graphon Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01358)
*Roland Boniface Sogan,Tabea Rebafka*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文提出根植于模体签名（rooted motif signatures）作为图论中图核（graphon）的高阶节点级表示，证明在有限秩图核中该签名能区分度函数无法区分的潜位置，并给出统计上的经验估计与浓度界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图核估计面临内在非可辨识性，现有的基于度函数的严格单调性假设过于严格，无法处理度函数为常数或非单射的情形，即使不同潜位置具有不同的连接轮廓。需要一种更强的高阶表示来区分这些位置。

Method: 引入根植于模体签名，记录每个潜位置上三角形、环、路径等局部子图模式的密度。对于有限秩图核，证明该签名可确定潜位置的连接轮廓；定义经验根植于模体签名并证明均匀浓度界。

Result: 对于有限秩图核，合适的根植于模体签名能区分不同潜位置的连接轮廓；但任意图核中仍存在不同潜位置具有相同签名的情况。统计方面，经验估计量满足均匀浓度界。

Conclusion: 根植于模体签名在度函数无信息时仍能揭示潜结构，是对基于度表示的有效补充，适用于常度数图核、非单射度函数及等块度的随机块模型等场景。

Abstract: Graphon estimation requires structural assumptions to address its intrinsic non-identifiability. A standard approach is degree-based identifiability, where the degree function is assumed to be strictly monotonic. This assumption is rather restrictive and fails for graphons with constant or non-injective degree function, even when distinct latent positions have different connectivity profiles. In this paper, we introduce \emph{rooted motif signatures} as higher-order node-level representations for graphons. They extend the degree function by recording, at each latent position, the densities of rooted motifs such as triangles, cycles, paths, and other local subgraph patterns. We study the extent to which these signatures can distinguish latent positions beyond degree information. For generic finite-rank graphons, we prove that suitable rooted motif signatures determine the connectivity profiles of latent positions. We also explain why such a property cannot hold for arbitrary graphons without additional assumptions, since different latent positions may have identical rooted motif signatures. On the statistical side, we define empirical rooted motif signatures from a single observed graph and prove uniform concentration bounds for these estimators. Simulation experiments illustrate that rooted motif signatures can reveal latent structure in settings where degree-based representations are uninformative, including graphons with constant or non-injective degree functions and stochastic block models with equal block degrees.

</details>


### [225] [Transferring supremum-norm rates and weak convergence of covariance kernel estimators to functional principal components](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01931)
*Hajo Holzmann,Kevin Wilk*

Main category: math.ST

TL;DR: 本文证明$L_2$扰动理论可将协方差核估计量的收敛率（包括上确界范数收敛和连续函数空间弱收敛）转移至相应的函数主成分（FPC）估计中。在固定同步设计的离散观测误差模型下，获得了FPC估计的最优上确界范数收敛率（含极小极大下界）及渐近正态性。稀疏到稠密转变现象同样适用于FPC，且特征值估计在稀疏设计下存在离散化主导区间。结果还适用于交叉协方差、长期运行协方差及导数过程协方差核的估计。数值实验采用Nyström方法计算FPC和特征值，并应用于日温度曲线序列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
